Every decomposition D = D_dom + r with D_dom <= PositiveTrace forces r >= -QW, so under the formal mainline theorem (hypothetical off-line zero -> qw(g) < 0) the map-108 boundary is forced > 0 and the registered kill condition fires BY FORCE for every owner; the owner-specific factorization reopen condition is unreachable. 2026-09-27 records 2028/2029/2030/2031, Route-B Cut-1 full assault (prereg 2028, outcome 2029, determinant margin 2030, sharp q ladder 2031). Owner throughout: rho = 0.55 + 14.134725141734693i, N = 4, L_p(s) = L_smoothSeed(s/2)^10 (the record-1980/1981 known-zero under-approximation). VERDICT P1 COUPLING-COLLAPSES + CONDITIONING-BLOCKS, with an ERRATUM that reaches back into two committed records. (1) dxi = 0.05 is UNDER-RESOLVED: the prime kernel sum 2*Lambda(k)/sqrt(k) cos(2*pi*xi*log k) resolves only log k <= 1/(2*dxi) = 10 while n = 4 needs log k = 12, so the whole n >= 1 column of results/1981_powered_seed_underapprox.json is a resolution artifact and not a reading: converging the grid moves n = 1 by 9.2e-3, n = 2 by 9.3e-2, n = 3 by 9.5e-1 on C (a factor 19.6 on C and 143.7 on D), and FLIPS THE SIGN of C, b and D at n = 4 (C +3.000119e6 -> -5.017356e5). Channel decomposition at n = 4, dxi 0.05 vs 0.02: the arch channel is resolution-stable (-1.63669e5 -> -1.64007e5) while the prime channel moves +3.1638e6 -> -3.3773e5, so the defect is entirely in the oscillatory channel, exactly as the prereg section 6 pre-registered. The grid is converged: dxi in {0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001} agree to <= 1.4e-13 on C, b, D, det at every n = 0..4 (dxi = 0.02 carries <= 7.3e-7). Consequences: 1981's 'gate signs at n = 0 and n = 4' becomes n = 0 only; record 1982's n = 4 gate row, lambda = 1437.9887 and the 2.573e-6 tail proxy are WITHDRAWN; map 106 sections 30/31 are marked superseded and a section 32 records the batch. (2) The prime-power book kills the n-currency: P_n = #{k prime power <= exp(2(n+2))} is 24/98/465/2532/15040/93371/595877/3878366 at n = 0..7 against the committed rig caps Ap <= 4000, B <= 60000, A <= 3000000, so the certified route set {Ap, B} empties at n = 5 and n = 7 has no route at all; the converged tail proxy 2.14e28 / 2.93e27 / 9.23e26 / 9.56e25 / 1.00e25 / 6.37e23 / 2.78e26 decays 8.04x per step, so a (1/2)-closure needs n ~ 31 > 4. Registered verdicts COUPLING-COLLAPSES (gate pattern fails on certified n = 1..4) and CONDITIONING-BLOCKS at n = 5 both fire. (3) P2 determinant margin, exact Fraction solve of the map-106 section 4 certificate: detUpper < 0 is reachable on every certified row, needing only eps < 3.82e-3 .. 9.03e-2 uniform relative moment accuracy (0.4142 = sqrt(2)-1 if the moment is centred at the vertex rational, quantization 1/2^20 booked inside U0/V0), which is LOOSER than Route A's health-witness price 1/(1+f) = 1.16e-5 .. 1.96e-2 (records 2010/2013). The determinant is the cheap half; the unreachable half is the sign pair, because on the certified rows n = 1..4 the CENTRES have C0 < 0 and b0 < 0, so Clo = C0 - eC <= C0 < 0 for every nonnegative eC. (4) P3 sharp q ladder: exact seed facts T(1-u) = 1-T(u) gives integral_0^1 T = 1/2, S_seed(0) = 3, seed0 = 3^10 = 59049 (the committed float 59048.99999985875 is quadrature error) and D_1 = 2, D_2 = 8 exactly (D_3..D_7 = 78.7283, 1266.22, 34570.6, 1377559.3, 83080389.6). k-fold integration by parts gives |S_seed(a+iy)| <= W_k(a)/|a+iy|^k with the LOCATED mass W_k(a) = integral_{-2}^{2} e^{ax}|f^(k)| = e^{-2a}A_k(a) + e^{2a}A_k(-a), computed by Gauss-Legendre at 40 digits. Because the node product has degree 26, the bound behaves like |t|^{26-10k}: only k >= 3 is uniform in t (k = 1 is 8.4e7 and k = 2 exceeds q from t ~ 360). At k = 3 the max over sigma in [0,1], |t| >= 28 is 3.0016e-07 at (sigma,t) = (0,28) against q = 2^-14 = 6.1035e-05, a 203x margin, decaying to 2.0e-18 by t = 800; with the crude e^{2|a|}D_k mass the same k = 3 bound is 5.9189e-05, only 1.03x under q. R-B1 is therefore PRICED VIABLE and the binding Route-B obstacle moves to R-B3 (no admissible n >= 1 gate row), not to the contraction premise. The batch also fixes two instrument-annotation defects: the 2028 prereg listed three routes {Ap,B,A} while the implementation produces only {B,A} (the Ap cap is dead code and the JSON field certified_routes named a route never measured), and a re-run of scripts/fourpoint_powered_seed_1981.py reproduces the committed JSON only to 8.7e-13 relative (worst at rows/4/det, not bit-exact), so the committed bytes were restored and the rerun kept as build-logs/20260927_1981_rerun_copy.json. 2026-09-27 record 2020, COVER currency desk (commit 80a5758e, pushed). Direction-C pricing executed as three labeled post-hoc diagnostics over committed rows — no new cell measured, no gate sign, no promotion, no RH claim. (1) BOOK IDENTIFICATION: the visible prime book is an exact function of the scale coordinate — np(s) = #{n prime power : n <= exp(2*s*m_pool)} with m_pool = max family width at s = 1 (gate anatomy: support_radius = 2*max_width, every width = scale*pool entry) — matched 27/27 committed cells (9 slots x 3 scales); this upgrades record 2017 s3c's "n_primes depends only on (layer, height, scale)" from measured invariance to identity, and shows the book moves with the HEIGHT too (m_pool 3.40 at gamma_1 vs 3.80 at gamma_2..gamma_6, same layer, same scale: 106 vs 182 primes). Book granularity: step spacing in scale 3.4e-3 (gamma_1), 2.0e-3 (gamma_2..6), 1.17e-4 (EXT) — 1-2 orders below the 0.01 grid. (2) BOOK STEP WEIGHT: C is a residual of a 4.5-order (committed) / 1.6-order (EXT) prime-vs-archimedean cancellation; the book's largest term (n=3, 83% of the whole prime channel at committed gamma_1@0.88) never steps inside the window; the terms that DO step are boundary primes: a SINGLE crossing moves C by <= 0.574|C| (committed, 4 crossings per 0.01 cell, aggregate <= 1.86|C|) and <= 0.0686|C| (EXT, 227 crossings per cell, aggregate <= 10.6|C|). Consequence: pointwise-in-scale gate claims are book-exact and finite; interval claims must carry the crossing terms and cannot inherit the sign from endpoints. (3) COMB AS SAMPLER: sign(C) along scale at delta = 0.10 is white at the 0.01 grid — flip rate 80/171 = 0.468 (C) and 70/171 = 0.409 (healthy) against independence references 2*mu*(1-mu) = 0.494/0.424, FLAT in h (0.01..0.04; the independent five-point floor grid reproduces R(0.02): 0.438/0.469 on disjoint pairs); pooled class COMB-WHITE. So record 2016's KNOT_COMPLEX is the run structure of a near-white sequence, not unresolved narrow bands; the healthy MEASURE is mu = 0.306 pooled over 180 certified cells -> H = 3.27 (per height 1.64..21.0), and because the grid behaves as a fair sampler these are unbiased estimates at the grid scale. RULING: at delta = 0.10 the 0.01 window currency is measured absent, the measure currency is measured, and the operative requirement is pointwise-in-scale certification at the measured cancellation depth (F-d cost unchanged: the 426 barrier's n equals the book size at the hitting scale). C1 REGISTERED (scale ladder: committed gamma_1, committed gamma_4, ext gamma_5; scale in [0.86,0.96]; steps 0.005/0.002; verdicts SAMPLER-ALL-RESOLUTIONS/WINDOW-LADDER/ALTERNATING-LADDER/INSTRUMENT-FAIL): the direct test of the fair-sampler reading at the coarsest resolution where committed-layer crossings are individually resolvable. M1/M2 RUNNING as record 2019 (pre-registration c9cde2ef): M1 = 189 cells at dxi = 0.002 chained to M2 = refined delta grid; M1 smoke reproduced the four shared cells bit-identically and RP-COMPLETE (r = 1.2e-06..3.7e-05); the dxi axis is 5+ orders less liquid than the scale axis (0 flips at fixed scale in the smoke), so the sign of C is a scale fact, not a quadrature fact. Rig hygiene this wave: round gamma ONLY for lookup keys, never before the family machinery (family_for_g keys the main group on abs(imag - g_main) < 1e-9, so a 6-decimal gamma drops the main node into the kill pool and silently shifts every family width — caught by 27/27 MISMATCH in the first boundary probe); after fixing a diagnostic script, RE-RUN it and trust the artifact's mtime, not the memory of a "final run" (the boundary artifact on disk was one run stale and read np_measured = None everywhere). No gate sign, no Lean brick, no RH claim.

2026-09-17 record 1590, WAVE V CONT-14 (paper + numerics, ZERO Lean bricks, zero new
declarations; commit `6c54b1b`), **THE CARRIER BASE IS ATTACKED HEAD-ON: NOT REFUTED,
NOT PROVED, BOTH CHEAP ROUTES CLOSED, OBLIGATION RE-TYPED.** (1) ERRATUM to 1331 §2.1:
its claim that φ is "analytic and nonvanishing in the interior of each half-plane"
contradicts its own pole placement in the same section; computed by hand and with
mpmath, φ = A/B (A = Γ_ℝ(1/2−2πiz), B = Γ_ℝ(1/2+2πiz)) has a ZERO at `+i/(4π) ∈ ₊`
and a POLE at `−i/(4π) ∈ ₋` (zeros at `+i(4k+1)/(4π)`, poles at `−i(4k+1)/(4π)`), so
φ is analytic in `ℂ₊` WITH zeros there and carries its poles in `ℂ₋`; the useful
statement is **`1/φ = B/A` analytic in `₋`** (B's poles are in `ℂ`; `1/A` is ENTIRE
because Γ_ℝ has no zeros). 1331 §2.2's conclusion "w is entire" STANDS; its stated
reason is replaced, and that conclusion is the first structural fact the whole face
rests on (EVERY carrier element is an entire function). (2) BLOCKED TRIVIALITY: the
cheapest refutation of `W ≠ {0}` is the two-sided rigidity `H²(ℂ₊) ∩ H²(ℂ) = {0}`
(available in the committed model: `P⁺ + P⁻ = id`, `PP⁻ = 0`), applied to
`W := (1/φ)(φw)`. It dies at the last step and the reason is quantitative: on the
horizontal lines of `₋`, `|1/φ(x−iη)| ~ (1+|x|)^{2πη}` — POLYNOMIAL GROWTH, matched
numerically against the committed factor to ratio 1 (rows at η = 0.0796, 0.1592 and
x = 5, 500) — so boundary values stay in `L²` while the shifted-line `L²` masses can
diverge; analyticity + `L²` boundary values does NOT imply `H²` (witness `exp(−z²)`),
and a second check killed the Liouville escape: ENTIRE functions CAN lie in `H²(ℂ₊)`
(witness `((sin z)/z)²·e^{2Iz}`: boundary `sin²x/x² ∈ L²`, line mass
`~ π/(2y³)(sinh²y·e^{−2y})² → 0`). (3) RE-TYPED: the base obligation is a nonzero
ENTIRE `w` with `w ∈ H²(₊)`, `φw ∈ H²(ℂ₋)` — a de Branges space element, classically
the Sonin/Weil model space (1331 §2.5), classical nonzero witnesses = products of two
Weil Λ factors; the productive route is T4 of `docs/proofs/1003` (prolate /
Connes–Moscovici negative eigenfunction), every intermediate step an unformalized
theorem. A full triviality argument was drafted and then SELF-REFUTED at the
weighted-`L²` step (this is why the counterexample family is in the record).
**Law F34**: a rigidity identity does not survive a multiplier — compute the
horizontal-line growth of `1/φ` before invoking it. Bones unchanged:
StripDensity(Λ) / EndpointMass(ε) / T1 OPEN; (★)/B4/ρ5/R4/(OB)/W1 OPEN. RH NOT claimed.

projections, no commutativity (1586 §1 in general form; its proof consumes the

project pair and in `⊓`-projection membership form; (c) `sub_sourceSonin_
Projection_eq_add_defect` + squared twin — 1587 §2.1 EXACT,

(F8/F31/F32 by shape): Mathlib has no fixed-space-of-sandwich lemma and no

angle of (range E, range Q) positive ⟺ range E + range Q closed` — in the

2026-09-17 record 1585, WAVE V CONT-9 (paper-only, zero Lean, zero digits, no
sentinel), **ERRATUM + STRIP-ESCAPE IDENTITY — the gate's residual re-priced
onto a finite strip.** (1) ERRATUM to 1584 §4/§5: "multiplication by the
compact-window indicator IS Hilbert–Schmidt with ‖M‖²_HS = Λ" is VOID — on
L²(ℝ), multiplication by a positive-finite-measure indicator is an orthogonal
projection of INFINITE RANK, hence not compact, hence not HS; composing with
the source inclusion `J` does not repair it (`‖M_χ∘J‖²_HS` = the carrier's
diagonal density integrated over the strip, finite only if the carrier is
thin). Same error class as the 1583 §3 |W|-bound that 1584 itself retracted.
Consequences: 1584 §4's constant-Λ bound and §5's 2Λ angle-compact bound are
WITHDRAWN (angle-compact part NOT closed); §5's tail bound
`Tr_carrier(E_{>L+2Λ})` is the compression of an infinite-rank projection ⇒
vacuous. (2) WHAT ACTUALLY CLOSES hradial (committed, quoted): the
`ContinuousKernelHilbertSchmidt` mechanism on COMPACT windows — continuous
kernel on a product of compact intervals ⇒ L² kernel ⇒ HS
(`C1G8R3CompositeBoundaryEnergy.lean:841-898`), with bounded pre/post
composition preserving column summability
(`PositiveTrace.summable_normSq_precomp/postcomp`) — plus the committed
`compositeRadialLeg_sourceBasis_normSq_summable` (:911-922) which consumes
`hwide`, which the transport ledger DELIVERS at λ′ = λe^{−Λ}. hradial status
UNCHANGED (CLOSED-PENDING-ONE-BRICK), mechanism corrected; no density input
needed there. (3) NEW EXACT STRUCTURE: left transports preserve the
Fourier-support class — `H(T_{−c}g) = T_c(Hg) ∈ E` for `Hg ∈ E`, `c ≥ 0`, so
`g ∈ range Q ⇒ T_{−c}g ∈ range Q`; escape from the carrier is purely RADIAL.
Hence `(1−P)T_{−Λ}f = (1−E)T_{−Λ}f + A E T_{−Λ}f` exactly (orthogonal terms),
with `(1−E)T_{−Λ}f = T_{−Λ}(f·1_{[L,L+Λ)})` — an exact STRIP identity of width
Λ, replacing 1584's infinite tail. (4) Re-priced residual = CARRIER THINNESS
FUNCTIONAL `Thin(Λ) = ∫_{[L,L+Λ)}K_P(u,u)du + Σ_i‖A E T_{−Λ}e_i‖²`; decisive
fork (F30): finite density ⇒ priced ⇒ hgap closes ⇒ (★) for the physical
factors; infinite ⇒ the compression route is structurally re-typed. Checked:
NO committed dimension/rank/density/localisation statement about
`ccm24ArchimedeanSoninClosedSubspace` exists — the thinness input is genuinely
new mathematics. Bonus committed anchor: `K_S = (E−R_S)∘Q∘(E−R_S)` (:168-183).
Nothing machine-checked. RH NOT claimed. Commit 89dc765.

(Q3 answer) the remaining content is a joint density/atoms condition on the BM intervals, critical scaling l ~ d^{(1-kappa)/2} (log-divergent at equality), so the obstruction is the shortness sum and not a pointwise ceiling; (rig) scripts/conjugate_budget_1632.py at 30 digits: ramp h = t 1_[1,T] closed form checks against quadrature to 1e-30 outside the support and gives ht(T+delta) = (T/pi) log(1/delta) + C with C = 18.5108 constant to 1e-5 (so L^1(dPi) alone gives no Lipschitz control - the hypothesis is a real restriction); the power model h = t^beta 1_[t>1] has ht = cot(pi beta) x^beta + O(1), deviation constant to 3 digits at beta = 0.25/0.75 (ceiling ATTAINED, Lemma 3 sharp) with the single exceptional exponent beta = 1/2 where cot vanishes and ht decays like x^{-1/2}; (class certificate) at z = i(8n+1)/(4 pi) the orientation m has a ZERO (|m| ~ 0.314 d) and m(-.) has a POLE (|m| ~ 318/d) with |m(iy) m(-iy)| = 1, so no entire factor makes the symbol inner in either orientation and Theorem B plus the inner-U remark are structurally closed to us - the base's class route is closed for a reason, not for a phrase; 2026-09-18 docs/proofs/1627_entire_w_obligations_two_corrections.md : route record - the elementary L2 face of the carrier base (T'): one nonzero g in L2 with supp g in [0,inf) and supp (U_m g) in (-inf,0], exactly V_arch(1) != {0}, stated in committed vocabulary (Radial, H, U_m) with no H2/PW layer; ONE PROVED OBSTRUCTION: no witness W can have finite exponential type (from W/A in H2(C_+) the weighted boundary L2 bound plus subharmonicity gives |W(x)| <= C e^{-pi^2|x|/2}|x|^{pi-1/4} on R, and a finite-type entire function with exponential decay on R has compactly supported transform analytic across the real axis, hence is zero), so every band-limited / Paley-Wiener / finite-type candidate is excluded and the witness must be infinite type (the Gamma-factor/prolate class of 1590); TWO CORRECTIONS: (i) the carrier is a conjunction of two half-line support conditions on the same vector and is genuinely scale-dependent - in the model m = 1, V_arch(lambda) != {0} exactly for lambda < 1 and no single translation maps it to lambda = 1 (the two endpoints must move in opposite directions), so 1624 section 4's unification of the B4 premise with the carrier base is withdrawn in its equivalence form (they share the symbol m only); (ii) a closed form drafted this round, A*B = pi^{1/2}/sin(pi/4 - pi^2 I z), is FALSE - counterexample Gamma_R(1/2)^2 = 7.4163 vs sqrt(2 pi) = 2.5066, the slip being Gamma((1-s)/2) != Gamma(1-s/2); the correct statements are A*B = pi^{-1/2} Gamma(1/4 - pi I z) Gamma(1/4 + pi I z) (hence 1/(A B) entire, zeros exactly at the poles of A and B, the "product of two Weil factors" object, which is too large: its quotient by A has modulus rho^{-3}) and Gamma_R(s) Gamma_R(-s) = -2 pi/(s sin(pi s / 2)) verified at s = 1/2 to 5 digits; MP criterion still not explicitized (precise statement in the 2005 paper, not retrieved; the surveys state the structural results only); carrier base OPEN, T4/B4/S3 unchanged and OPEN, no witness, RH not claimed.

## 14. CardinalRaw derivative/L1 recurrence (1964)

Added C1ExplicitSmoothSeedDerivativeBudget.lean and its paired audit. The module proves the actual one-step bound

l1Mass (derivativeShift f a) <= derivativeL1 f + ||a|| * l1Mass f

and the exact list recurrence shiftedProductL1Bound, yielding l1Mass_cardinalRaw_le for the real node list (nodes.erase z).toList. Owning build and paired audit completed successfully in WSL ext4; audit axioms are exactly propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound. This is a genuine budget interface, but not yet a numerical owner-level bound: derivativeL1 of the explicit seed/product and node-product separation remain open.


## 15. CardinalRaw denominator separation interface (1964 follow-up)

Extended the checked derivative-budget module with norm_nodeProduct_self_ge_pow. For any actual finite owner and any nonnegative separation delta satisfying delta <= norm(t-z) for every t in nodes.erase z, Lean proves delta ^ (nodes.erase z).card <= norm(nodeProduct nodes z z). The owning build and paired audit pass with standard axioms only. This turns the denominator issue into the concrete owner-level separation obligation; it does not supply a zero-separation theorem or a numerical delta.


## 16. Batch closure of seed/cardinalRaw mass budgets (1965)

Added C1ExplicitSmoothSeedMassBudget.lean and its paired audit. The explicit seed satisfies l1Mass smoothSeed <= 4 from support [-2,2] and pointwise amplitude <= 1. For any complex a, the exponential weight satisfies l1Mass (exponentialWeight smoothSeed a) <= 4 * exp(2 * |a.re|). Both owning and paired-audit builds pass with only propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound.

Extended C1ExplicitSmoothSeedDerivativeBudget.lean with the exact budget consumer l1Mass_cardinalRaw_le_of_budget. It accepts a scalar budget recurrence for the actual suffix list and returns the corresponding bound for cardinalRaw without changing owner order. Owning and paired-audit builds pass with standard axioms only. An attempted stronger length/product closed form was rejected and removed because its recurrence inequality did not follow; the exact budget recurrence is retained.

The remaining quantitative OPEN is now narrower: supply explicit derivativeL1 bounds for the concrete smooth seed and every shifted suffix, plus actual zero-node separation delta. These inputs feed the correction L1 and hcorrectionQuadratic budgets; signed determinant and same-index tail remain OPEN.


## 17. Support-times-sup derivative budget (1966)

Extended C1ExplicitSmoothSeedDerivativeBudget with derivativeL1_le_of_support_of_norm_le. If the actual derivative is supported in [-B,B] and has pointwise norm at most M >= 0, Lean proves derivativeL1 <= (2*B)*M. The owning build and paired audit pass with only the standard three axioms. This does not invent a numerical M for smoothTransition; it isolates the remaining analytic task to an explicit pointwise derivative bound.


## 18. expNegInvGlue derivative constant (1967)

Added C1ExplicitSmoothSeedDerivativeConstants.lean and its paired audit. Using the exact Mathlib derivative formula for expNegInvGlue and the elementary inequality t^2 * exp(-t) <= 4 for t >= 0, Lean proves norm(deriv expNegInvGlue x) <= 4 for every real x. (4) Sign census over 36 rows: C > 0 on 11/36, b > 0 on 7/36, and the map-106 section 2 endpoint (C>0, b>0, det<0) IS realized at two knob points -- (delta=.05, gamma_1, n=0, k=30, scale=.90): C=+5.054485, b=+2.964940e4, D=-8.044092e7, det=-1.285676e9, lambda_n=5866.0, gate=det/C=-2.5436e8; and (k=40, scale=1.0): C=+0.9844515, b=+2.305039e3, det=-1.231690e7, lambda_n=2341.4, gate=-1.2511e7. (5) In the regime C>0 with D<0 the determinant is AUTOMATICALLY negative, so section 2's binding obligations reduce to C>0 and b>0 alone. (6) No robustness mechanism exists in the free knob: b flips sign between adjacent scan knots (scale .86 -4.5e3, .88 -2.0e4, .90 +2.96e4, .92 -7.2e3), so any Lean endpoint must come from the construction's analytic structure. (7) A Cut-2 witness (some nonzero lambda with Q(lambda) <= 0) exists on 36/36 rows across three sign regimes; the only pattern with no witness, C>0 and D>0 with det>0, is unobserved. (8) Instrument: three prime-channel routes with the certified pair (Ap = per-prime trapezoid on the 8x spline-refined xi-grid, B = direct kernel with the prime sum excluded so that arch + prime_B is not a double count); the pair is bit-stable in dxi and converges at exactly dxi^4 (4.4e-07 down to 6.6e-12 relative over dxi = 8e-03..5e-04); route A (dual FFT plus spline interpolation) is excluded as interpolation-limited (agrees with the certified pair only to 2e-02 on C after the phase fix); the 1919 variance identity reproduces the channel-split determinant at rel <= 1.2e-11 on refined grids, and on the coarse grid its residual is bounded by spread_C * |CD/det| (verified: 22 * 5.4e-05 = 1.2e-03). Laws recorded in the proof record: F80 the dual-FFT phase must be exp(-2 pi i xi_0 x_k) read at the ACTUAL dual-grid frequency and must be calibrated on an ASYMMETRIC grid (a symmetric grid hides the sign error; wrong sign = 6.25e-06, naive raw real part up to 2.0 relative, and on the real owner it flipped C from +5.05 to +10.09); F81 a variance/moment identity is homogeneous of degree 2 in the grid weights, so feed the measure K*W*dxi, not the integrand K*W, else det is inflated by 1/dxi^2 and reads as a bogus 1e4..1e6 "relative error" (a units error, not a numerical one). Cut-2 remaining obligations unchanged and OPEN: prove C>0 and b>0 for the ACTUAL committed base/correction, the section 2 determinant sign as a theorem, the joint smallness budget beta_s * L_n < multiplicity_rho * lambda_n^2 at lambda_n ~ 2.3e3..5.9e3, and a computable admissible base with a certified contraction constant. No gate sign, no determinant sign, no producer witness, no RH claim.

2026-09-25 route verdict recorded with 1968 (analysis only, no Lean brick, no record): proving C > 0 analytically from arch + prime channel estimates is a DEAD END and should not be re-litigated. Reasons, in order of force. (1) The target is a relative cancellation at the 1e-3 level between two channels of magnitude ~700 that are computed by different mechanisms; no estimate built from separate channel bounds can certify a sign under that condition, because the error budget of the sum exceeds the object being bounded. (2) The archimedean symbol satisfies sigma(2 pi xi) > 0 only for |xi| < u*/(2 pi) ~ 1.0009, while the committed-owner density peaks at |xi| = 1.6..3.6 (1959 readings), so on the peak region the arch channel alone is negative. (3) A log-2 window contains no visible primes, so there C = arch_C < 0 outright. (4) The sign has no robustness: C > 0 on only 11 of 36 rows and flips between adjacent knob settings, exactly as b does (1959: b = -4.5e3, -2.0e4, +2.96e4, -7.2e3 at scale .86, .88, .90, .92). Consequence for the plan: C > 0 must be produced by CONSTRUCTION - a designed/certified admissible base with the positivity built in - and never read off channel sign bookkeeping or an instrument scan. Consistent with 1959's closing line that the endpoint must come from analytic structure. Handed forward on the seed side: sharpen the seed derivative constant 4 to 2 (budget 16 to 8) by splitting into the disjoint windows (-2,-1) and (1,2), where the passive factor equals 1 with zero derivative; then the shifted-product recurrence constants and the node-product constants, both of which multiply this base.

Pitfalls paid this round (also added to root AGENTS.md section 5): (a) le_or_lt does not exist in this Mathlib - it is le_or_gt; (b) pow_le_pow_left₀ takes the EXPONENT explicitly (pow_le_pow_left₀ h0 h1 6) - leaving it out leaves a metavariable the elaborator cannot guess; (c) mul_div_cancel_left₀ takes the VALUE first and the nonzero hypothesis second (mul_div_cancel_left₀ b (ne_of_gt h)); (d) intervalIntegrable_const leaves IsLocallyFiniteMeasure ?m stuck unless the measure is pinned (intervalIntegrable_const (μ := volume)), and intervalIntegral.integral_mono_on takes hab/hf/hg as leading explicit arguments; (e) differentiableAt_id has fully implicit arguments - use (hasDerivAt_id v).differentiableAt; (f) abs_add does not exist: build the triangle steps as abs_le.mpr with neg_abs_le / le_abs_self / neg_le_abs; (g) an unannotated 0 defaults to Nat INSIDE norm_num goals (pow_le_pow_left₀ with a Nat-typed zero still proves - and proves the WRONG statement, silently), and a positivity hypothesis without an annotation is Nat-typed too - annotate (0 : ℝ) in every hypothesis; (h) the ratio linter wants spaces around / inside numeric literals (1 / 10, not 1/10) and the empty-line linter fires on a blank line between a standalone comment and the following declaration - merge the comment into the section key instead of leaving it floating; (i) a standalone last line without a trailing newline re-triggers the '' starts on column N style warning, and a scratch file edited through a Windows-side writer needs the trailing newline restored every round; (j) a python cleanup pass that re-scans its own output while splitting lines explodes the file (one while-loop pass turned 1959 lines into ~5763) - split by exact shape, never iteratively, and keep the pre-cleanup green copy to restore from. Open: the higher rungs j >= 4 (same certificate method, higher-order profile), the final assembly of the certified rungs into the committed consumer's budget, the node-product constants, the strip contraction, the numeric cardinalRaw budget at a concrete node set, the correction quadratic margin, the signed determinant and the joint tail margin, with C > 0 still to come from a designed admissible base on the construction side. No gate sign, no determinant sign, no RH claim.

2026-09-26 records 2006/2007/2009/2010: Route A health-cone shape scan (rank-spread direction scan over the feasible fibre). 2006 pre-registered 4 owners x 8 ranks of 17 x 4 amplitudes + anchor = 132 rows at dxi=0.008 on the support-preserving two-copy 0.75a/a family. Two instrument amendments were needed before the registered verdict was valid. 2007 diagnosed the smoke INSTRUMENT-FAIL: (a) det_B is built with np.trapezoid while the record-1919 engine receives the rectangle-weighted grid measure mu=(sig+Kp)*W*dxi, so det_B = det_var is a quadrature agreement, not an algebraic identity (the flags I1 5e-3 and I3 1e-6 were unsatisfiable); (b) the registered certification test reads only spread_D, so a row whose C sign is route-dependent could still be certified and counted healthy; (c) the route-keyed readout is the Ap route, and the four-owner resolution ladder (calibration script, artifact md5 3858b81234700940f189309290d36078) shows C_B is resolution-stable to <=2.3e-08 relative across dxi in {0.004,0.008,0.016} while C_Ap drifts by up to 3.7e-01, so the Ap error is proportional to the channel magnitude and dev_C is unbounded relative to a small committed C. Restated: I1a wiring (dxi=0.004 reproduces the record-2003 anchors to 0.0e+00 on C and D for all four owners), I1b gate dev_D <= 1e-3 (measured <= 2.215e-05), I1c dev_C reported not gated, I1d trace identity |A-C_B|/max(|C_B|,1) <= 3e-3 (measured <= 4.02e-04 over the run), I3a |det_var-det_moment|/max(|det_var|,1) <= 1e-9 (measured <= 5.82e-13), I3c det gap <= 5e-3 (measured <= 8.71e-04). Health predicate amended to the route-robust conjunction (C>0,D<0,det<0 on BOTH the route-keyed and the route-B readouts). 2009 records the first full run (2474 s, all 128 rows certified, ident OK, INSTRUMENT-FAIL only on the G5-W dev_C band), restates I1 as above, and refuses to move the registered resolution to 0.004 because N=14 sits against the H-CONE-FAT threshold of 16 and several pairs sit exactly at radius 0.05 (a resolution change after seeing N would make the verdict resolution-tunable); it also pre-registers the reproducibility expectation. 2010 is the outcome of the re-executed registered scan: VERDICT H-CONE-STRATIFIED/RANK-FLAT/MECH-MIX, run 2 equals run 1 bit-for-bit on C/B01/D/det/W0 over all 128 rows (worst deviation 0.0e+00, no flag mismatch). Health radius: G5-H none except 0.02 at ranks 6/12/14/17, G5-W none anywhere, G7-H 0.05 at ranks 1/2/4/9/12/17 and 0.20 at rank 6, G8-H 0.80 at ranks 2/4/6 and 0.05 at ranks 1/9/12/17; N=14 pairs at sigma>=0.05. Only three (owner,rank) pairs increase the margin with the perturbation, all G8-H and all energetic (ranks 2/4/6, factors 3.20/4.26/4.04); every other pair collapses, most through a sign flip. The registered RANK-FLAT label is a rule artifact (argmax over ratios that are all negative returns the least-collapsed rank). MECH-MIX: f=mm/A changes by +2.3%/+36.2%/+130.9% on the three increasing rows. D<0 in 128/128 rows on both routes, including all 104 rows where C has flipped; healthy_ap = healthy_b = the conjunction on all four owners (4, 0, 16, 21), so no healthy row depends on the route. Rank 14 loses health at the first amplitude 0.02 for all four owners. Conditioning finding: f=mm/A at the committed anchors is 7196 (G5-H), 86087 (G5-W), 133 (G7-H), 51 (G8-H), so the committed A is the residual of a cancellation of order f; the obligation D<0 is well conditioned (certified-route spread <= 6.0e-04) while the health witness C>0 is not (spread up to 6.9e-02), which is why the gamma_5 owners have no cone. Route-A numeric exposure sits in the admissibility check, not in the obligation. Not core progress: no bound on the selected detector, no new no-go, no smaller obligation; record 1926 stands, TAIL/COVER untouched, no RH claim. Route README (001_route_a_signed_kernel) and map 104 updated.

(2) P4c (scripts/fourpoint_support_radius_robustness_2034.py, results/2034_support_radius_robustness.json): the same rows at support radius 2(n+2)+delta, delta in {-1,+1,+2}, RADIUS-FRAGILE - exactly ONE of the 15 rows (gamma_2, N=5, n=2) keeps the pattern at delta = +1 and +2; 14 of 15 move (delta = -1 truncates below the construction's own radius, so it is inadmissible and recorded as a sensitivity direction only). (3) P4b (scripts/fourpoint_gate_tail_pairing_2033.py, results/2033_gate_tail_pairing.json): same-index pairing tau = A0 q^(2n) (1 + H^4/|lambda_n|)^2 with A0 = H^4 (2 pi)^12 (C4 C2)^2 from the record-2028 decay block (heights 0..200, 401 points, sigma in {0,1/2,1}); anchor: the q = 1/2 control reproduces record 2028 to 2.22e-16 (record 1981's withdrawn dxi = 0.05 lambda values are NOT read, anchor dev 0.808 expected); PAIR-MISMATCH with paired_rows = [] because on EVERY gate row tau_inf = A0 q^(2n) is already above 1 (gamma_2 n=2 2.115e10; gamma_3 n=2 7.447e12/8.511e12/9.668e12; gamma_3 n=3 2.774e4/3.171e4/3.602e4), so the vertex factor (1+H^4/lambda)^2 >= 1 cannot rescue it and NO span coefficient closes the tail there; the tail first closes at n = 4 (tau_inf 2.9e-7 .. 1.3e-4, tau < 1 at the measured vertex lambda for gamma_1 and gamma_2) where the gate pattern is gone, so the gate index and the tail index are DISJOINT on this family; forcing the pair at the n = 2 gate rows needs q smaller by 381x (gamma_2) to 1763x (gamma_3) than the priced 2^-14, and 5.5x .. 5.7x at the gamma_3 n = 3 rows; derived closure index n_min = ceil(log(A0)/(2 log(1/q))) = 3 at gamma_1 and 4 at gamma_2/gamma_3, prime books 2532 and 15040, both inside the rig cap 60000, so the old n ~ 31 wall does NOT reappear under this pricing. (4) P5 (artifact blocks lambda_screen / lambda_joint_rows): the vertex-annotated parabola is D - lam*B + lam^2*C with B = 2b, while the rig JSON stores b (rig gate = det/C = D - 2 lam b + lam^2 C), so the two conventions agree EXACTLY and no mismatch exists - recorded explicitly so nobody re-derives a false one; LAMBDA-PAYS holds on the C < 0 rows only (8 rows, log10 lambda windows [0.5,30] and [5.0,30]), where the gate opens downward and the Lean consumer exists_pos_lambda_quadratic_neg_of_det_neg (hC : 0 < C, hB : 0 < B) cannot be met, and on every C > 0 gate row the joint set is EMPTY because tau_inf >= 1; record 2029 reopen condition 2 therefore closes in the negative direction. (5) P6 (scripts/fourpoint_interval_mass_2033.py, results/2033_interval_mass_w3.json, STATUS MASS-BRACKETED): a genuine interval bracket for W_3(a) = e^(-2a) A_3(a) + e^(2a) A_3(-a), A_3(a) = integral_0^1 e^(a u)|T'''| du, mpmath.iv at dps 40; 20000 equal panels, each sign-certified by the mean-value enclosure T'''(u) in T'''(m) + (u-m) T''''([p,q]) (19728 panels) or charged the rigorous envelope (q-p) sup|T'''| (272 panels, all with true mass below 3.3e-6 against a total of 39.4), the two end pieces [0,1e-6] and [1-1e-6,1] carried as 1e-60 slack plus a 1e-12 relative conversion cushion; the bracket contains all four record-2031 binding values (|a| = 0.025/0.225/0.275/0.525 -> 78.982934/83.630285/85.992335/105.834383) at relative width <= 5.0e-03, and the strip bound recomputed with the bracket UPPER end on the full registered 101 x 345 grid is 3.001847221e-07 against q = 2^-14 = 6.103515625e-05, margin 203.33x, only 6.9e-05 relative above the uncertified record-2031 value 3.001641057e-07. Ledger: R-B1 is priced AND mass-certified, R-B2 cheap and no longer binding, R-B3 is the binding wall with its obstruction changed from "no admissible n >= 1 gate row" (retired) to "the gate index and the tail index are disjoint" - a quantitative index-scale mismatch (the budget falls by q^2 = 2^-28 per unit n and crosses 1 one step after the gate dies), scoped to this family; R-B0/R-B4 are prerequisites (record 2034). Pitfalls paid this round (also added to root AGENTS.md section 5): (a) mpmath interval endpoints are ivmpf, NOT mpf - .a/.b must pass through mpf() before any mpf arithmetic, otherwise "can only create mpf from zero-width interval"; (b) the panel builder must NOT recurse on straddling panels - the mean-value enclosure straddles zero on both neighbours of a simple root AND across the whole underflow region (u <~ 0.01, u >~ 0.99), so the recursion branches to the width floor and never finishes (a stale process burned 22 minutes of CPU before being killed, and its log line was the tell that it was running the superseded script); (c) near u = 1 the interval T = 1/(1+exp(hD)) has width ~1e-40, T - T^2 then straddles zero, and the hD powers amplify that width by u^{-12}-scale factors - the panel u = [0.999949, 0.999999] carried an envelope mass of 1.18 against a true value near 3e-30 (3% of the whole mass) until the mirror T^(k)(1-u) = (-1)^(k+1) T^(k)(u) was used to evaluate every panel with p > 1/2 on its mirror inside (0, 1/2); (d) a point scan for the roots is polluted by the same cancellation (spurious sign changes reported at u = 0.9895..0.9999); (e) an a-grid must cover the ARGUMENT RANGE OF EVERY LOOKUP, not the range of the scanned variable - the grid [-0.57, 0.31] covered the scanned a but not its negation, so the a = -0.525 cell silently fell back to the last grid value and UNDER-estimated an upper bound; the grid is now [-0.60, 0.60] and both lookups RAISE outside it instead of degrading; (f) record 2032's log prints dev_C against the PREVIOUS anchor row (cosmetic off-by-one in the anchor loop; the JSON values are correct); (g) history preservation: the committed record-1981 JSON bytes must not be re-derived (a rerun reproduces them only to 8.7e-13, kept as build-logs/20260927_1981_rerun_copy.json) and the dxi = 0.05 rows stay withdrawn. Files: docs/proofs/2032_full_block_assault_preregistration.md, 2033_gate_tail_pairing_preregistration.md, 2034_support_radius_robustness_preregistration.md and 2035_route_b_full_block_outcome.md; scripts/fourpoint_gate_row_search_2032.py, fourpoint_gate_tail_pairing_2033.py, fourpoint_support_radius_robustness_2034.py, fourpoint_interval_mass_2033.py; results/2032_gate_row_search.json, 2033_gate_tail_pairing.json, 2033_interval_mass_w3.json, 2034_support_radius_robustness.json (plus the labelled firstpass-uncorrected non-reading); docs/map/README.md, docs/map/103, docs/map/106 section 33, the Route-B README (live screen state, candidate n = 2/3) and the route 004 execution status. Not core progress: no Lean theorem, no bound on the selected detector, no new formal no-go (the R-B3 mismatch is a numeric family obstruction on an under-approximate owner), no strictly smaller formal obligation; no gate-sign theorem, no interval certificate for the gate moments, no RH claim.
## 2026-09-27 — Route B current-family freeze

Route B's same-owner four-point family is now recorded as
`DEAD_ON_CURRENT_FAMILY`. The committed under-approximation gate rows occur at
`n = 2, 3`, while the same-index tail is impossible there and first becomes
closable at `n = 4`; therefore the gate index and tail index are disjoint. The
result is a scoped family no-go, not a global impossibility theorem for every
future four-point construction. Do not continue interval gate certification or
Lean work on the current rows. Reopen only after a named-hypothesis probe
produces a complete-owner gate at or beyond the tail-closure index, or an
all-strip q bound strong enough for the actual gate row and lambda.

## 2026-09-27 — Route D theta-shadow defect domination candidate

Created Route D as a new representation-level candidate. It uses the
corrected same-owner trace identity
`PositiveTrace = QW + D` and targets an owner-specific contraction
factorization `D = Tr(A^* C A) + boundary` with `0 <= C <= I`, so that
`QW = Tr(A^*(I-C)A) >= 0`. This is narrower than global Weil positivity and
preserves the actual selected detector and finite visible-prime owner.

Record 112 rejects the universal projection/phase-algebra shortcut by an
exact 2-by-2 example: the defect is positive on a direction where the square
vanishes. The corrected trace identity still depends on unproved analytic
trace/readback hypotheses. An owner-specific factorization using actual
arithmetic/theta data remains an untested sketch, not RH progress. No Lean
consumer, parameter scan, or producer theorem is licensed. Record 113 then
performed the first-brick algebra: with `P T P = -2 P P_hat P`,
`D = PositiveTrace + (1/2) Tr(X T)`, so `D <= PositiveTrace` is exactly
`QW >= 0` and hence `ICgate <= 0` under the existing readback. Route D's
current abstract factorization is therefore frozen as no-progress; only a new
owner-specific arithmetic identity can reopen it.

## 2052 — Route A link L1: enclosed nodal values (committed-evaluation
forward bound)

**Verdict: ENCLOSED-L1-L2-VIABLE** — the model part's nodal input, the last
addend charged as zero (L1) and the last MEASURED addend (the 2048 float
slack `5.536e+10`), is now a PROVEN charge.  Verdict line: aggregate
`5.938659e+17` (2051) + L1 `1.884216e+13` + arch `3.917065e+16` =
**`6.330553e+17 = 0.0633x budget`**, no zero-charged and no measured addend
left.  Probe `scripts/routea_l1_nodal_enclosure_2052.py`, artifact
`results/2052_l1_nodal_enclosure.json` (md5 ffbdc5e912edb74c933fae4a321aaafe),
record `docs/proofs/2052_route_a_l1_nodal_enclosure.md`.

**Scope / calculus.**  Bound on the committed float64 evaluation of g at the
ladder nodes vs the stored-floats-exact object O (2051 scope).  Committed DAG
per node: `s = 0.5 - 2 pi i xi`; `v_j = a_j (exp(z_j X_j) @ (phi W_j))`;
`lb = base @ v`, `cc = corr @ v`; `p = Re(Prod_m (cnt_m - s))`;
`g = ((p p) |lb|^2) |cc|^2`.  Entries carry `e_v_j = (EPS_TERM + m U +
AMP_j) M_0_j INS` (m = 2400 GL nodes; EPS_TERM = 16u; the SAME 2051 AMP
constant `8u a^2 (0.5 + 2 pi XI_MAX + |theta|)`), matvecs
`gamma_17 S + Sum_j |w_j| e_v_j`, products with SAFE magnitudes
`X_s = |x| + e_x` and `e_z = X_s e_y + Y_s e_x + U X_s Y_s`; global insurance
INS = 1.05.

**Charge.**  `charge_L1 = Sum_j Coef1_j e_g_j + Coef1(h) ECHO_REL max_j |C_j|
INS` with `Coef1_j = Sum_k c_k |W_j(k)|` (tent weights, cross-read to 2048
rel 1.6e-08 at h = 0.002) and ECHO_REL = 1e-15 (the 2048 dg_reference rate;
DISCLOSED proxy, impact ~9.3e+08).  Ladder: `1.847135e+13 / 1.875201e+13 /
1.883083e+13 / 1.884216e+13` at h = 0.01/0.005/0.002/0.001 = 333.7x / 338.7x
/ 340.2x / 340.4x the superseded slack and 1.88e-06 of budget; `e_g <=
1.357e-09 |C|_max` (`|C|_max = 3.7906e+19`), e_g median 3.082e-19
(cancellation floors, h-independent); per-node charge 64x below the flat
`Coef1(h) e_g_max = 1.200e+15`.  `dg*` positive only at h <= 0.002
(3.093e+14 / 4.016e+14).

**Anchors (all pass).**  B1 containment 6/6 vs an mpmath dps-40 exact-object
re-evaluation (worst ratio 6.1e-05 at the mass peak xi = -6.68; 9.5e-11 at
the xi = 0 core; measured discrepancies at the float floor everywhere); B2
Coef1 vs 2048 rel 1.60e-08; B3 ladder-vs-fine nodes BITWISE equal (numpy
linspace lands on the 1e-03/2e-03 fine grid exactly -- the 2051 A1 residual
1.47e+05 was evaluation, not coordinates); B4 positivity/sum identity rel
<= 9e-16; B5 forward-calculus selftest vs mpmath dps 50 with input errors
inflated to 1e6 U rel (0/200 violations, worst ratio 0.952); B6 cross-read of
the 2051 artifact worst_rel 0.0.

**Incidents.**  (i) B5 first build failed 200/200 (ratios to 8.8e+33): the
selftest's exact chain had an extra square `(LR^2+LI^2)^2` -- the INSTRUMENT
failed, not the bound.  (ii) B1 failed at the xi = 0 cancellation core by
26.85x: the calculus used the FLOAT value as the magnitude where the true
|c| is bounded by `|c_float| + e_c` (true |cc| 5.9e-14, float 1.04e-20, e_cc
5.9e-09), losing the `e_cc^2` floor (3.8e-17); fixed by SAFE magnitudes ->

**Finding.**  The 2048 slack was a magnitude-budgeted MEASUREMENT, not a
bound; rigor on this addend costs 340x yet 1.9e-06 of budget.  Instrument
laws in `AGENTS.md` §2c.  Remaining L2: L4 (separate), L5 (upstream
ideal-vs-stored, registered).  Not a producer theorem; not RH.

bar10 alone, 7.85x under), sigma 1.4%, nodal 0.03%.  Each lever alone is

interval projection (arch_proj = sum_i w_sigma * g_mid * 0.05,
refinement-proof at ~|sigma'| dxi = 9.287269e+11) is replaced by a
per-panel THIRD-ORDER Taylor model of int sigma*g: moments G_k by
trapezoid on the fine grid with (dxi^2/12) int |g^(j)| error charges
from the model's own sup enclosures on the 0.001 grid; sigma^(k)(m) and
sup_panel|sigma'''| from the termwise-differentiated Bernoulli ladder
psi'(w) = 1/w + 1/(2w^2) + sum B_2n w^{-2n-1} (+T1; psi'', psi''' the
same pattern) + exact shift sums psi^(k)(z) = psi^(k)(z+8) +- J_{k+1},
tails from the Gauss representation bounded at |T0| <= 2 M13/(|w|^12
Dmin), M13 = |B_14|/28 = 1/24, T1..T3 by Cauchy on the disk rho = x/2,
all x SAFE = 8 (validation peak 0.93 pre-SAFE at u = 0).  Reading:
arch3 = 6.072137e+08 one-sided around centre -2.347126e+14 (relative

3.478784e+10 + charge_kernel 3.607401e+04; charge_value = (SIG_MAX +

**Three levers, all three defects, all now instrument laws (`AGENTS.md`
§2g).**  (1) The (P) outer-panel elementary bound now takes the max of
the PRODUCT `phi(x) e^{s'x}` via `h_out`: h(u) = -K/(1-u^2) + a Re(s) u
is strictly concave on (-1,1) (h'' = -2K(1+3u^2)/(1-u^2)^3 < 0), so the
panel max is the clip of the unique root of h' (80 bisections).  The
drafts' product-of-maxima form was the binding term of the whole price;
gain 1.49x (a = 1.76) to 43.1x (a = 4.752), el_max 4.2586e-18 ->
9.8802e-20, and the S3b a-side incumbent moved 2.73e-08 -> 3.410e-13.
(2) The S7 quadrature is the EULER-MACLAURIN corrected trapezoid
(`int f = T_h - (h^2/12)(f'(b) - f'(a)) + R`, `|R| <= (h^4/720) int
|f''''|`): the drafts charged `(h^2/12) int sup_cell |E''|` through the
|.|-sum error slots, which carry no cancellation (smoke means err2
3.7e16 vs err0 0.73), read 3e+07x the trapz term and priced L5 at
1.336e+18.  The EM endpoint form reads EM 4.05e-09 + regress 3.62
against the trapz 7.5068e+07; `sigma_loc = min(2, 2 err0/(|g|+err0))`
damps the K4 tail (k4_trapz 3.25e+20, ek4_trapz 2.61e+15).  (3) P6 now
scales by the SUM of the two evaluators' own certified allowances
(`EG_r52 + jet err0`): worst 9.906e-06 over 16 shared nodes, where the
drafts' safe-magnitude S6 scale read 2.265e-07 (S6 bounds VALUE
magnitudes, not accumulation rounding, whose scale is the per-family
absolute sum ~1.7e-12).

**Measured structure of (P).**  The elementary outer-panel bound is
|Im s'|-INDEPENDENT and dominates at EVERY node; all four inner
Trefethen panels are floored at 1e-300 (rho^{-2m} = e^{-5575} at
uc = 0.5, m = 1600), so `el_end == el_hump` for all 17 families and
`rule == rule_wsup` (7.128255e+07 both).  The node-dependent machinery
(rule_bound_vec at every node, the (2 pi a^2)^k derivative transport,
A11 rel 0.00e+00) is kept as the honest evaluation of the same formula.

**Controls.**  All gates pass: C1 Q400 frozen, C2 Q1600 rel 2.4e-15, C4

**Registered lever (for the next record).**  The binding (P) channel is
a bound on |I| + |GL| (the SUM, not the residual), oscillatory-unaware
and m-independent.  Split-rule bridging: `|GL - I| <= |GL - GL_split| +
|GL_split - I|` with the outer panels subdivided at |u| = 1 - delta --
every piece is either ellipse-bounded or a thin edge slice carrying

scale (~1e-12 relative).  Expected >= 1e+03.  L4 (2053) standing,
evaluator-independent; not a producer theorem; not RH.

(grid controls rel 1.195e-06 / 6.388e-07 phi-shift; a CONTROL, not an
enclosure) + qe_arith 1.143069e+05 (INS (int_e0 + int_ecm) + 32U
trapz|W dg|; int_e0 5.443122e+04, int_ecm 5.443251e+04 -- routing, not
cast; 32U replaces the tight 20U) = 1.402572e+05 = 5.405x |dQ| = 1.032x
the KKT dc_base 1.359110e+05.

**Price.**  charge_L5 = 1.842946e+09 -> 1.104942e+08 (16.679x) =
charge_value 1.104582e+08 + charge_kernel 3.607399e+04; the 0.001-grid
split of int e_g is 3.738711e+06 solve + 2.380518e+05 ns (exact, D4), so
the solve part priced 463.4198 x 3.738711e+06 = 1.732593e+09 before and
1.402572e+05 now (12353x -- the kernel multiplication no longer applies
to it) while the F/X/(P) part is unchanged at 1.103179e+08.  Triangle
4.401166e+10 + 1.104942e+08 = 4.412216e+10 = 0.01295x budget3 = 0.1295x
bar10, margin 7.72x (2057: 7.43x).  Channels now: F/X/(P) back in the
binding position (99.87% of charge_L5; rule_bridge 1.950806e+05 = 81.9%
indicative, X 9.738743e+04, F 5.442840e+04); solve 0.127%.  S7 ns:
int e_g 2.380518e+05, err0_max 3.680363e+05 (2057: 7.884e+06), err0_med
2.787e-16, int_g 1.235172e+14 unchanged, ek4_trapz 8.561e+12 (2057:
1.208e+14).

**Why (the mechanism).**  The linear split is numerically dead: lin and
the second-order residual both scale as 1/h exactly (lin = dQ/h across
h = 0.008/0.004/0.002; linbound exactly doubling) because the Ab_j
integrals alias (family xi-oscillation up to 2 pi a X ~ 283 rad/unit)
while the pointwise difference W (g1 - g0) inherits the grid's
resolution; the bare pointwise-envelope analog reads 9.28e+06 = 405x dQ,
the 2057 RT+K4 form 3.79e+06 at 0.004 = 146x.  The construction matters:
a float solve of the same equations (cond(S) 3.766e+08) deviates from
the mp-exact interpolant by 1.9e-10 / 2.3e-10 relative (5.511e+03 /
3.040e+06 -- the d-sum scale), moving dQ by 12% (2.289397e+04 vs the
probe's 2.595018e+04) because dQ/Q0 = 7.6e-09.

**Controls.**  R57 reproduces the 2057 price IN THE SAME RUN, bitwise
(rel 0.0 on int e_g, charge_value, charge_L5); B7 zeroing of dbase/dcorr
is bitwise on the value slots (k4/ek4 and the int_g regress term move BY
DESIGN -- the solve channel's own K4 entry); D1 float-vs-mp pointwise
difference, 3 nodes, worst ratio 4.955e-06 (xi = -6.68); D3 envelope
monotonicity; D4 same-grid marginal reading 0.98565x the 2057 decomp;
B6/A12a/A12b/A13/P2 0.0/P6 9.906e-06/P3/P4 3.010e-16/A10 2.04e-53/A11
0.0/P5 floor/lmax 2.63e+04/A7/A8/A9/B3/B5/S8 -- 29/29 gates green.

**Laws (`AGENTS.md` §2i).**  Direct-difference law for
quadratic-function channels (never the derivative split -- aliased);
ideal-interpolant construction law (mp-exact min-norm cast, never a
float solve); computed-vs-bounded channel law (a computed difference
enters the charge additively, not through the 463.42x kernel constant);
pointwise-reference convention law (each object's own s-convention; lift
the float W -- P_from_nodes: s = -2 pi i xi, family values:
s = 0.5 - 2 pi i xi); error-slot zeroing law (gate the value slots); 
smoke-grid comparison law (compare the h-independent trapz);
post-chain accumulation constant 32U.

**Registered lever (for the next record).**  The rule_bridge channel's
computed-difference accumulation allowance (allow0_max 1.214e-23, 2057
§3); then the solve-side arithmetic channel (qe_arith 81.5% of
charge_solve; int_e0 and int_ecm agree to 1.000024 relative, so a
shared/cancellation-aware arithmetic chain is the natural cut, gain
bounded by the 0.127% share); then the kernel constant 463.4198 itself.
L4 (2053) standing, evaluator-independent; not a producer theorem; not
RH.
## 2026-09-28 — Route A subroute topology after 2058

Route A was reorganized under
`route/000_rh_mainline/002_b5_compactlog/001_route_a_signed_kernel/` so that
each distinct hypothesis has an explicit status: `001_uniform_panel_interval`
and `002_ibp_quadrature` are scoped dead architectures (records 2043 and
2046); `003_panel_local_l2_model` is active infrastructure only;
`004_selector_family` records selector-specific no-goes and parked branches;
`005_c3p_selected_owner` is the active core producer target; and
`099_frozen_or_audit_only` is evidence storage, not a campaign.

The next core attack is the signed C3' margin for the actual selected owner,
with the same detector, support, finite visible-prime set, and quantifier
order. The 2058 L2 result may be attached only to a matching error term. Do
not treat further L5 pricing as core progress unless it removes a named
model-to-real premise such as the Gram rule gap, the `a_mat` idealisation
gap, or COVER.

2026-09-29 record 2175 — strict pinned C3' determinant margin. The new
`C1C3StrictPinnedMargin` module proves, for every carrier frequency and every
healthy `CompactLogTest` owner, an explicit same-owner margin
`μ = (-ICgate narrowArchRoot.convolutionSquare) * ICgate g.convolutionSquare > 0`
with the exact two-span determinant at most `-μ`. The determinant/phase split
therefore gives the same strict margin for the aggregate Archimedean + mixed +
prime C3' budget; the cross channel is retained as an exact square. Owning
module, paired audit, and full `ConnesWeilRH` aggregate builds pass (4148 jobs)
with only `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` and no `sorryAx`. This is
owner-socket progress, not selected-detector closure: support, triple
vanishing, zero detection, and full spectral-tail compatibility remain open.

2026-09-29 record 2176 — explicit optimal q-form margin for the pinned C3'
owner. `C1C3StrictPinnedMargin` now defines the exact optimal two-span q-form
and proves the determinant product bound
`det <= -((-ICgate narrowArchRoot.convolutionSquare) * ICgate g.convolutionSquare)`.
The optimal-q theorem exposes the usable witness
`μ = -ICgate narrowArchRoot.convolutionSquare > 0` and proves the q-form is at
most `-μ` for every carrier on the demodulated root/healthy pair. This is a
strict quantitative owner-socket strengthening, not a selected-detector
producer closure or an RH result. Module, paired audit, and root aggregate
builds (`20260929_c3_qform_margin_build9.log`, `...build10.log`,
`...build11.log`) pass with only `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`
and no `sorryAx`; support, triple vanishing, detection, and full tail
compatibility remain open.

2026-10-01 record 2306: the 2304 flat-edge IBP tail rebuilt as a grouped directed instrument and TURNED LIVE. Verdict TAIL-SHARP-COVERED at artifact grade: TAIL-SHARP-COVERED means the best tail bound over the tested ladder is 2.145448788e-32 at N = 36 (margin 4.661029457099352e38 against the 1e7 hgap budget), every order N >= 16 clears the budget (N = 16: 553.7599646, margin 1.81e4; N = 20: 2.620431005e-6, margin 3.82e12), and only N = 8 (1.795497633e22) and N = 12 (2.605891575e12) are dead; the bound turns over past N = 36 because the u-space magnitude ratio grows with N faster than (2 pi)^{-4N} decays. The mechanism: h_f^{(N)}(y) = e^{lambda_f y} e^{-K/s_f} Q_f(y) with Q_f = sum_j C(N,j) lambda_f^{N-j} R_f^{-j} A_j(u) s_f^{-2j} evaluated DIRECTLY in the (u, s) variables with positive s-powers (a single log-scaled exp per j-term; no expanded y-polynomial), enclosed on 11277 rational cells by ub = |sum_f c_f t_f(m)| + kappa magchain + (Delta/2) sum_f |c_f| sup|t_f'| with honest triangle bounds: |A_j(u)| <= magA_j(u_max), sup_s e^{-K/s} s^{-m} = e^{-K/s*} s*^{-m} at s* = clip(K/m, s_lo, s_hi), and |A_j'| s^{-2j} <= (magA_{j+1} + 2 u_max (2j s_hi + K) magA_j) S(2j+2) from the exact ODE identity A_j' = (A_{j+1} - 2u(2j s - K) A_j)/s^2 (A generated to N+1). Why the rewrite: the expanded-polynomial form cancels catastrophically near |y/R| ~ 0.5 - measured coefficient-magnitude sums up to 1.37e16x the value at N = 20 (first instrument reading tail_bound 5.083377274e31 with inflation_base 1.21e9), while the u-space j-sum keeps the ratio at <= 8.3e4 worst family and the reread gives inflation_base 1.008 / inflation_corr 4.604 and tail_bound 2.620431005e-6 at the same order. Controls: kappa variation 2^-24 vs 2^-36 moves ub by 1.18e-8 / 1.11e-7 / 1.10e-7 at N = 20/24/28 (slack nowhere binding); the independent mpmath Bell-path recomputation at the best-order argmaxes gives ub/true = 4.84 on the base channel (tight) and 2.53e7 on the corr channel (loose locally, binding only where the margin is astronomical); feeding 2304's own rigorous V values through the 2306 tail formula reproduces 2304's recorded bounds to stored precision (ratios 1.0000001/1.0/1.0/0.99999999 at orders 16/20/24/28). Errata carried: 2304's v_ladder omits the 2 R_f integration-length factor (2306 carries one 2 Rmax on the grouped sup) and 2304's price arithmetic is 416.1x not 74x ((2.9973776e17/1e7)^{1/4}; 5450x with the length factor restored). Pitfalls caught by controls: the A_j recursion missing the 2u s leg (Bell closed form caught it at 12.7 rel error); the cell value carrying the cell-sup factor e^{y_hi/2} instead of e^{m/2} (mpmath value control showed an exactly uniform 5.000e-07 = e^{Delta/2} - 1 bias); a raw 1/s_lo^2 producing inf then inf*0 = nan at cells touching +-R (fixed by folding into S(2j+2)). The tail half of hgap is now numerically free at this grade; the live constraint on the 1e7 budget is the finite-window half (2295-2302 lane, sampled-grade proxies 0.0405x-0.0494x), which needs its own certified enclosure before any hgap certificate. Artifacts results/2306_hgap_tail_sharp.json, results/2306_order_{8..44}.json, results/2306_kc_*.json, results/2306_mp_control.json; record docs/proofs/2306_routea_hgap_tail_grouped_sharp.md; selftests scripts/routea_hgap_tail_sharp_selftest_2306.py (13 tests PASS) and the module MODE=selftest (PASS). LESSONS LOCKED (AGENTS §2aa): (i) ivmpf.a and .b are DEGENERATE INTERVALS (ctx.make_mpf((a,a))), not raw mpfs — their sum can widen when a + b is not exactly representable, so float((x.a + x.b)/2) raises ValueError; use .mid (mpi_mid at ctx.prec, degenerate by construction) for float readouts; (ii) the soundness direction of a TIGHTENED majorant is measured against the zero-padding stored-jet witness, NOT the model-grade T — budgets below model errors put the certified T strictly BELOW the model T, so "certified >= model" was a sign error that failed den64 at ~6 min per attempt and cost one full ladder relaunch before the wall was recognised as a control-design bug; (iii) an iv-scale unit must be built as mp.iv.mpf(2)**-k (a plain mp.mpf mixed into iv arithmetic raises "can only create mpf from zero-width interval"); (iv) mp.iv.mpf([lo, hi]) is the list-form constructor for re-wrapping an interval, there is no two-argument form; (v) the extended-jets polynomial tensor is (chunk, length, 2) — index polynomial[:, degree, :] before broadcasting, not [degree]; (vi) ladder run hygiene: never relaunch without TaskStop of the previous background task and a pgrep -af sweep (two writers on one log spliced tracebacks that read like code corruption; per-rung log files prevent recurrence). # 2319 — 2026-10-01: P-only readback blocks direct identification

The exact four-point annihilator multiplier used by the spectral-prefix route
vanishes at the marked centered orbit pair.  The selected healthy detector's
Hermitian square has values `-1` there from the raw `1/-1` readback, so the
P-only square cannot replace the selected negative detector.  The fixed-cutoff
tail factor `4*C*(3/4)^N` only supplies a gain comparison when the actual-owner coefficient to
marked-gain ratio is controlled; coefficient growth `(4/3)^N` cancels the
geometric decay.  Record 2319 stores the exact Python source/readback check,
self-tests, and the scoped no-go.  The first import-wide build exposed
pre-existing `C1HealthyYoshidaSpectralNegativity` errors at lines 371 and 385,
with equal offending source hashes across the authoritative tree and mirror.
The focused readback imports only the detector and selected-owner definitions
and builds independently; no unrelated source was repaired. Actual-owner
growth is not instantiated: the P-only marked gain is zero for every cutoff.
Final acceptance: focused module/audit Build completed successfully (3627
jobs), zero errors, no new-source warnings or sorryAx; all 12 audited leaves
have [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]. Root control Build completed
successfully (4148 jobs), zero errors; this does not cover every optional leaf.

# 2320 — 2249/2308 kernel scope mismatch

The first executable scope audit found that the 30 owner families are bitwise
identical, but the support formulas differ: 2249 uses `2 * max(width)` and
produces 52 prime powers through 167, while 2308 uses `2 * max(width^2)` and
produces 41136 prime powers through 492467 (cutoff 492475). The 2249 margin
therefore cannot be transferred to the selected-square physical kernel by
family or coefficient hash. The required next object is the omitted-book
contribution from 167 to 492475, evaluated and enclosed on the actual selected
owner. Artifact: `results/2320_kernel_owner_scope_audit.json`.

# 2321 — omitted prime-book cancellation dominates

On the same 30-family owner and 4001-point grid, the 2308 full prime book
changes the 2249 short-book functional by signed `+1.554582542873e8`, but a
pointwise absolute omitted charge is `2.225576909988e16`, about `1.3285e4`
times the full signed value. Termwise triangle bounding is therefore a scoped
no-go for the omitted-book lane. The next proof mechanism must preserve
prime-power cancellation by grouped summation or a certified oscillatory
quadrature. Artifact: `results/2321_omitted_prime_book_diagnostic.json`.

2321 follow-up: xi-panel grouping preserves more cancellation but remains too
loose at practical fixed widths. The absolute panel sums are 3.49e15, 1.42e15,
7.52e14, 2.77e14, and 1.50e13 for widths 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, and 2.0;
the width-2.0 result is still about 9x the 2249 margin. Use oscillation-aligned
adaptive panels or certified higher-order quadrature, not fixed-width triangle
bounds.

# 2322 — Fourier-side decomposition preserves the useful cancellation

For the omitted prime book, integrate the selected-owner weight against each
prime-power cosine before summing. The sampled sum of absolute Fourier terms is
6.216173118934e8, while the pointwise product charge is 2.225576909988e16 and
the signed total is 1.554582535574e8. This leaves about 2695x margin against
the current 1.675396e12 bar. The next certified object is a per-frequency
Fourier coefficient enclosure with quadrature and finite-window remainder,
not a pointwise kernel envelope. Diagnostic only; no producer GO.

# 2323 — Fourier refinement control

The same owner at dx=0.02 and dx=0.01 gives signed omitted deltas
155458254.287 and 155457647.398 (movement about 607), and Fourier absolute-term
sums 621617311.893 and 621593325.402 (movement about 23986, relative 3.9e-5).
This supports the Fourier interface but the movement must be charged; grid
refinement is not a proof.

# 2324 — owner-weight forward error budget

The 2249 forward-error shadow propagated through the actual owner weight into
the omitted Fourier coefficients sums to 1.179864458159e9, below the current
1.675396046388e12 margin by about 1420x. The remaining binding obligation is
Fourier coefficient quadrature plus endpoint/finite-window remainder; do not
count sampled refinement as that proof. Artifact:
`results/2324_fourier_owner_error_budget.json`.

# 2325 — quadrature trust horizon

Independent GL controls found a severe alias regime: GL8x160 gives an absurd
2.58e17 abs-term sum, GL16x160 gives 3.74e13, GL16x320 gives 6.2166e8, and
GL16x640 gives 6.2159e8. The GL16 panel refinement movement is 3.7439e13 then
1.2537e5. Use panel width <= 0.25 for the next coefficient enclosure; GL
exactness count is not an accuracy claim. Endpoint and remainder terms remain
open. Artifact: `results/2325_fourier_quadrature_probe.json`.

# 2326 — same-panel order refinement

At fixed panel width 0.25 (320 panels), GL16 to GL32 moves the Fourier
absolute-term sum by 1.254236610300e5 and signed total by -1.752111274390e4,
matching the GL16 x 320 to GL16 x 640 spatial refinement scale. This confirms
the diagnostic trust horizon through two independent controls, not a proof of
the analytic remainder. Artifact: `results/2326_fourier_order_refinement.json`.

# 2327 — cosine-only remainder headroom

For GL16, panel width 0.25, the standard remainder prefactor times the sampled
owner-weight maximum and the omitted frequency 32nd-power sum gives 3.2513e4,
only 1.94e-8 of margin. This is partial only: W derivatives, endpoint
correction, and full-line tail remain uncharged. Next derive an owner-weight
derivative majorant rather than promoting the cosine-only number.

# 2328 — global product derivative majorant no-go

After fixing an initial double-GL-weight bug, the global product derivative
majorant is 9.235602894507e23, about 5.512489368958e11 times the margin. The
failure is structural: taking absolute basis derivative moments before owner
coefficient combination destroys cancellation in L_base/L_corr. Freeze this
global triangle route. The next admissible mechanism must use panel-local or
interval-jet evaluation that preserves owner cancellation. Artifact:
`results/2328_global_product_remainder_probe.json`.

# 2329 — panel-local cancellation-preserving jet

Combining basis derivatives with selected owner coefficients before taking
absolute values reduces the complete Leibniz product proxy to 7.472203544915e5
(4.459962503209e-7 of margin), versus 9.235602894507e23 for the global
triangle. The first W^(32)-only proxy was discarded; the recorded value
includes all W^(k)*phi^(32-k) terms. This is center-jet diagnostic only;
next add panel interval radius inflation. Artifact:
`results/2329_panel_local_jet_probe.json`.

# 2330 — panel radius feasibility

The cancellation-preserving jet was sampled at all 320 panel centers and 640
endpoints. The complete product proxy rises from 7.4722e5 to 1.0045e6, only
5.996e-7 of margin. This supports panel-local jets but is not an interval
proof; endpoint sampling must be replaced by a certified local radius.
Artifact: `results/2330_panel_radius_probe.json`.

# 2331 — panel-local Lipschitz inflation

An order-33 local jet with sampled first-order panel-radius inflation gives a
complete product proxy of 2.521425870721e6, 1.504973033784e-6 of margin. It is
about 2.5x the endpoint probe and remains cheap. This is not an interval proof;
replace sampled maxima with a proved local radius next. Artifact:
`results/2331_panel_lipschitz_inflation.json`.

# 2332 — panel-local Taylor enclosure feasibility

The local jet was extended through order 34. For 320 panels of width 0.25 and
radius 0.125, the complete product remainder proxy is 1.133133770579e7,
6.763378563666e-6 of margin. This explicitly models panel radius with a
Taylor tail and remains viable. It is not yet a certificate: stored float
basis moments, directed intervals, endpoint correction, and full-line tail are
still open. Artifact: `results/2332_panel_taylor_enclosure_probe.json`.

# 2333 — forward-shadow Taylor tail

The usable 2333 implementation replaces slow scalar mpmath interval calls with
2249 forward-shadow product inflation plus math.fsum/SUMCHARGE accumulation.
The Taylor-tail proxy remains 1.133133770579e7, 6.763378563666e-6 of margin,
matching 2332. The scalar mpmath attempt was stopped without artifact and is
not evidence. Formal center-jet intervalization and endpoint/full-line terms
remain open. Artifact: `results/2333_forward_tail_budget.json`.
